# Skills · D · part 230

822 entries, dyadic-attack-path-analysis to dynamical-systems. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **dyadic-attack-path-analysis**: Trace a supplied security candidate from attacker-controlled source through parsing, identity, policy, state, and controls to a security-sensitive sink and impact. Use when reachability or exploit conditions are unclear; do not use for broad candidate discovery, final report prose, or live exploitation beyond the authorized validation boundary. ([HiLleywyn/dyadic-skills](https://github.com/HiLleywyn/dyadic-skills/tree/HEAD/dyadic/security/dyadic-attack-path-analysis))
- **dyadic-catalog-maintenance**: Maintain the separate lley.cat skills, agents and plugins repositories, preserve Dyadic snapshot provenance, build reviewed import packages and report runtime compatibility gaps. Use for catalog publication and synchronization, not a Dyadic production deployment. ([HiLleywyn/dyadic-skills](https://github.com/HiLleywyn/dyadic-skills/tree/HEAD/dyadic/operators/dyadic-catalog-maintenance))
- **dyadic-current-work**: Revise accepted work in Dyadic while preserving the owner’s constraints, exact file versions and unresolved items. Use for a follow-up that changes an accepted delivery or needs a Patch against it. ([HiLleywyn/dyadic-skills](https://github.com/HiLleywyn/dyadic-skills/tree/HEAD/dyadic/workflows/dyadic-current-work))
- **dyadic-decomposition-and-induction-on-scales**: Dyadic decomposition, pigeonholing a good scale, induction on scales. Use when a quantity is easy at each scale but summing the $O(\log N)$ scales is the difficulty, when spillover between scales is the obstruction, or when a polynomial-size quantity should be $N^{o(1)}$. ([fcescob/terence-tao-skills](https://github.com/fcescob/terence-tao-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/dyadic-decomposition-and-induction-on-scales))
- **dyadic-extension-authoring**: Create or update Dyadic skill bundles and specialist presets, or document implemented plugins and MCP services without widening runtime authority. ([HiLleywyn/dyadic-skills](https://github.com/HiLleywyn/dyadic-skills/tree/HEAD/dyadic/iris/dyadic-extension-authoring))
- **dyadic-finding-discovery**: Discover plausible security findings in a pinned repository or component and record source-linked candidates for later validation. Use during the candidate-generation phase of an authorized assessment; do not use it to publish unvalidated scanner matches, assign final severity, or perform uncontrolled live exploitation. ([HiLleywyn/dyadic-skills](https://github.com/HiLleywyn/dyadic-skills/tree/HEAD/dyadic/security/dyadic-finding-discovery))
- **dyadic-finding-validation**: Skeptically validate or disprove supplied security candidates against a pinned artifact using source review, counterevidence, and the safest sufficient reproduction. Use before reporting, severity assignment, fixing, or issue creation; do not use to generate a broad new candidate list. ([HiLleywyn/dyadic-skills](https://github.com/HiLleywyn/dyadic-skills/tree/HEAD/dyadic/security/dyadic-finding-validation))
- **dyadic-fix-finding**: Implement a narrow, authorized source change for a validated security vulnerability and add focused regression coverage while preserving intended behavior. Use only when the user explicitly asks to fix a specific finding; do not use for speculative hardening, ordinary bug cleanup, deployment, or unrelated refactoring. ([HiLleywyn/dyadic-skills](https://github.com/HiLleywyn/dyadic-skills/tree/HEAD/dyadic/security/dyadic-fix-finding))
- **dyadic-receipt-reconciliation**: Explain interrupted or apparently contradictory Dyadic runs by separating execution state, recorded tool effects, accounting state and provider cost. Use for operation IDs, stopped runs, unknown charges or parent/child usage comparisons. ([HiLleywyn/dyadic-skills](https://github.com/HiLleywyn/dyadic-skills/tree/HEAD/dyadic/workflows/dyadic-receipt-reconciliation))
- **dyadic-security-diff-scan**: Review a pull request, commit, branch comparison, or working-tree patch for security regressions introduced by the change, including affected callers and tests. Use for change-focused security review; do not use it as a substitute for a repository-wide audit or to report pre-existing vulnerabilities as introduced by the patch. ([HiLleywyn/dyadic-skills](https://github.com/HiLleywyn/dyadic-skills/tree/HEAD/dyadic/security/dyadic-security-diff-scan))
- **dyadic-security-hardening**: Develop prioritized architectural and operational security hardening from validated findings, incidents, threat models, or assessment evidence. Use for defense-in-depth and systemic risk reduction; do not present absent best practices as confirmed vulnerabilities or implement broad changes without an explicit build request. ([HiLleywyn/dyadic-skills](https://github.com/HiLleywyn/dyadic-skills/tree/HEAD/dyadic/security/dyadic-security-hardening))
- **dyadic-security-policy**: Create or revise a repository SECURITY.md or component security policy covering supported versions, reporting channels, response expectations, safe-harbor testing boundaries, data handling, and disclosure. Use when maintainers want an actionable policy; do not infer legal promises, bounty eligibility, or authorization beyond terms the owner supplies. - **dyadic-security-scan**: Perform a standard single-pass security assessment of a repository or scoped component in Dyadic, producing validated findings, negative results, immutable snapshot identity, and an honest coverage receipt. Use for an audit or security scan of source as it exists, not for a diff-only review, exhaustive repeated deep scan, patch implementation, or unauthorized live penetration test. ([Rheinmir/setup](https://github.com/Rheinmir/setup/tree/HEAD/.overstack/doyourmagic/nuwa-skill/skills/dym-nuwa-skill-fidelity-scorecard) · ★11)

- **dymad-train-eval-workflow**: Use DyMAD MCP tools to register datasets, inspect schemas, translate natural-language model/training requests into structured config, train and evaluate models, then export a reproducible CLI config/run manifest so users can audit, edit, and rerun with the package-level dymad CLI. ([smanist/dymad-dev](https://github.com/smanist/dymad-dev/tree/HEAD/skills/dymad-train-eval-workflow) · ★1)
- **dymad-tuning-convergence-study**: Build concrete DyMAD tuning/convergence studies that use package-owned tuning and convergence primitives, create study-specific problem modules and reproducible CLI wrappers, and add workflow smoke tests. Use when a user asks an agent to implement, adapt, or review a freeform convergence study involving custom targets, sampling, models, fitting/scoring, tuning specs, or convergence plots. Do not use for ordinary DyMAD MCP train/eval workflows. ([smanist/dymad-dev](https://github.com/smanist/dymad-dev/tree/HEAD/skills/dymad-tuning-convergence-study) · ★1)
- **dymos-trajectory**: Use when setting up and assessing pseudospectral trajectory optimization with Dymos: plan optimal-control problems as phases with collocation nodes, check that phase setup includes initial-state and final bounds plus an objective, and verify convergence, state continuity across segments, and total delta-v against expected budgets for ascent or orbit-transfer trajectories. Flags under-resolved phases (fewer than 5 nodes), unconverged runs, and discontinuities at segment boundaries. Trigger: trajectory optimization, optimal control, dymos, pseudospectral, phase, convergence, collocation, launch… ([ashfordeOU/aero-agent-skills](https://github.com/ashfordeOU/aero-agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/gnc-autonomy/optimal-control/dymos-trajectory) · ★8)

Dùng khi người dùng nói "tạo form", "dựng form đăng ký/khảo sát/…", "sinh JSON form", "build form dynaform", "thiết kế form bằng AI". Dùng khi field cần danh sách từ API, option phụ thuộc field khác, địa chỉ API phải đổi theo môi trường, hoặc điền sẵn giá trị.
